Palaga's AI Agent","__typename":"User"},"body":"`VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.ResponseHandler` can overwrite a completed decoupled SOAP response\nwhen the empty HTTP 202 acknowledgement for the original request is processed afterward.\nThe client then throws `ClientImpl$IllegalEmptyResponseException` despite having received a valid response.\nThe result should remain available regardless of the order in which the two responses arrive.\n\nThis defect was found while investigating the native WS-ReliableMessaging failure in\n[workflow run 43142815034, job 130933581016](https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/actions/runs/43142815034/job/130933581016).\nRelated integration tracking issue: #2293.\n\n- Quarkus CXF: `3.40.4-SNAPSHOT`, based on commit `b12c15a1f01cfa3e685a5be2f36efe9f7dc14f93`.\n- Quarkus: `3.40.999-SNAPSHOT`, commit `93c7ca2c408c97dcb5bd919a3e30fd0475dfb2cf`.\n- Apache CXF: `4.2.4`.\n- Native builder: Mandrel `25.0.5.0-Final`, JDK `25.0.5+8-LTS`; Maven/test harness used Java 17.\n- Failing test: `WsReliableMessagingIT.testTwowayMessageLoss`, calling `hello(\"Paul\")`.\n  Expected HTTP 200, received HTTP 500.\n  The JVM counterpart passed, and native compilation succeeded.\n\nThe application log shows Paul's request at `03:45:37.483`, a correctly correlated `helloResponse`\ncontaining `WS-ReliableMessaging Hello Paul! counter: 3` at `03:45:37.487`,\nand the following failure at `03:45:37.489`:\n\n```text\njakarta.xml.ws.soap.SOAPFaultException: Response message did not contain proper response data.\nExpected: {https://quarkiverse.github.io/quarkiverse-docs/quarkus-cxf/test/ws-rm}helloResponse\nCaused by: org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl$IllegalEmptyResponseException\n    at org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl.processResult(ClientImpl.java:675)\n```\n\nIn the empty/partial-response branch,\n[`ResponseHandler.handle()` unconditionally calls `exchange.setInMessage(inMessage)`](https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/blob/b12c15a1f01cfa3e685a5be2f36efe9f7dc14f93/extensions/core/runtime/src/main/java/io/quarkiverse/cxf/vertx/http/client/VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.java#L1467).\nThe preceding decoupled-endpoint check guards callback removal, but does not guard this assignment.\nIf the SOAP reply has already populated the exchange and marked it finished,\nthe late HTTP 202 replaces that reply with an empty message.\n`ClientImpl.processResult()` then finds no decoded result and throws the exception above.\n\nA focused local reproducer invokes the real compiled `ResponseHandler` and CXF 4.2.4 `ClientImpl.processResult()`\nwith controlled delivery order and stubbed HTTP objects.\nThe compiled handler's source was checked against the failing 3.40 commit and is identical.\nIt produces:\n\n```text\nOrdering: HTTP 202 -\u003e SOAP reply\nReply preserved: true\nCXF result: [WS-ReliableMessaging Hello Paul! counter: 3]\nOrdering: SOAP reply -\u003e HTTP 202\nReply preserved: false\nCXF failure: org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl$IllegalEmptyResponseException: Response message did not contain proper response data. Expected: {https://quarkiverse.github.io/quarkiverse-docs/quarkus-cxf/test/ws-rm}helloResponse\n```\n\nThis confirms the overwrite defect and reproduces the exact exception.\nAttribution of this particular CI failure to that ordering is strongly supported, but remains an inference:\nthe CI log does not trace the empty HTTP 202 handler or the exchange assignments.\nThe reproducer is a component-level diagnostic, not a native end-to-end reproduction.\n\nThe [November 8 run](https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/actions/runs/42432252214/job/128583513744)\nfailed on the same Paul call with the same exception.\nThe [November 11 run](https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/actions/runs/42940456485/job/130260744312)\npassed with the same Quarkus commit, consistent with a timing-dependent failure.\n\nProposed fix: prevent empty transport acknowledgements from replacing the application response\nfor decoupled exchanges, while preserving ordinary one-way behavior and reviewing explicit 202 propagation.\nAdd deterministic regression coverage for both arrival orders and concurrent delivery,\nthen validate `WsReliableMessagingTest#testTwowayMessageLoss` and\n`WsReliableMessagingIT#testTwowayMessageLoss` in JVM and native modes.\nNo fix has been implemented or validated yet.\n\nThe equivalent unconditional assignment is also present in\n[Apache CXF 4.2.4 `HTTPConduit`](https://github.com/apache/cxf/blob/cxf-4.2.4/rt/transports/http/src/main/java/org/apache/cxf/transport/http/HTTPConduit.java#L1724)\nand warrants an upstream fix.\n\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eStandalone component reproducer\u003c/summary\u003e\n\nSave as `Late202Reproducer.java`.\nCompile and run with the built `extensions/core/runtime/target/classes` directory and that module's\ndependencies on the classpath.\nThe package declaration permits access to the package-private response handler.\nThe reproducer deliberately expects the current defect; it must be adapted to assert preservation for a regression test.\n\n```java\npackage io.quarkiverse.cxf.vertx.http.client;\n\nimport id="code-view-repo-link" href="/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf" data-discover="true"></a><!--$--><!--$--><script type="application/ld+json">{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"DiscussionForumPosting","headline":"Decoupled empty acknowledgement coming (rarely) after the actual response propagated as the response to the incoming observer","articleBody":"`VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.ResponseHandler` can overwrite a completed decoupled SOAP response\nwhen the empty HTTP 202 acknowledgement for the original request is processed afterward.\nThe client then throws `ClientImpl$IllegalEmptyResponseException` despite having received a valid response.\nThe result should remain available regardless of the order in which the two responses arrive.\n\nThis defect was found while investigating the native WS-ReliableMessaging failure in\n[workflow run 43142815034, job 130933581016](https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/actions/runs/43142815034/job/130933581016).\nRelated integration tracking issue: #2293.\n\n- Quarkus CXF: `3.40.4-SNAPSHOT`, based on commit `b12c15a1f01cfa3e685a5be2f36efe9f7dc14f93`.\n- Quarkus: `3.40.999-SNAPSHOT`, commit `93c7ca2c408c97dcb5bd919a3e30fd0475dfb2cf`.\n- Apache CXF: `4.2.4`.\n- Native builder: Mandrel `25.0.5.0-Final`, JDK `25.0.5+8-LTS`; Maven/test harness used Java 17.\n- Failing test: `WsReliableMessagingIT.testTwowayMessageLoss`, calling `hello(\"Paul\")`.\n  Expected HTTP 200, received HTTP 500.\n  The JVM counterpart passed, and native compilation succeeded.\n\nThe application log shows Paul's request at `03:45:37.483`, a correctly correlated `helloResponse`\ncontaining `WS-ReliableMessaging Hello Paul! counter: 3` at `03:45:37.487`,\nand the following failure at `03:45:37.489`:\n\n```text\njakarta.xml.ws.soap.SOAPFaultException: Response message did not contain proper response data.\nExpected: {https://quarkiverse.github.io/quarkiverse-docs/quarkus-cxf/test/ws-rm}helloResponse\nCaused by: org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl$IllegalEmptyResponseException\n    at org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl.processResult(ClientImpl.java:675)\n```\n\nIn the empty/partial-response branch,\n[`ResponseHandler.handle()` unconditionally calls `exchange.setInMessage(inMessage)`](https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/blob/b12c15a1f01cfa3e685a5be2f36efe9f7dc14f93/extensions/core/runtime/src/main/java/io/quarkiverse/cxf/vertx/http/client/VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.java#L1467).\nThe preceding decoupled-endpoint check guards callback removal, but does not guard this assignment.\nIf the SOAP reply has already populated the exchange and marked it finished,\nthe late HTTP 202 replaces that reply with an empty message.\n`ClientImpl.processResult()` then finds no decoded result and throws the exception above.\n\nA focused local reproducer invokes the real compiled `ResponseHandler` and CXF 4.2.4 `ClientImpl.processResult()`\nwith controlled delivery order and stubbed HTTP objects.\nThe compiled handler's source was checked against the failing 3.40 commit and is identical.\nIt produces:\n\n```text\nOrdering: HTTP 202 -> SOAP reply\nReply preserved: true\nCXF result: [WS-ReliableMessaging Hello Paul! counter: 3]\nOrdering: SOAP reply -> HTTP 202\nReply preserved: false\nCXF failure: org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl$IllegalEmptyResponseException: Response message did not contain proper response data. Expected: {https://quarkiverse.github.io/quarkiverse-docs/quarkus-cxf/test/ws-rm}helloResponse\n```\n\nThis confirms the overwrite defect and reproduces the exact exception.\nAttribution of this particular CI failure to that ordering is strongly supported, but remains an inference:\nthe CI log does not trace the empty HTTP 202 handler or the exchange assignments.\nThe reproducer is a component-level diagnostic, not a native end-to-end reproduction.\n\nThe [November 8 run](https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/actions/runs/42432252214/job/128583513744)\nfailed on the same Paul call with the same exception.\nThe [November 11 run](https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/actions/runs/42940456485/job/130260744312)\npassed with the same Quarkus commit, consistent with a timing-dependent failure.\n\nProposed fix: prevent empty transport acknowledgements from replacing the application response\nfor decoupled exchanges, while preserving ordinary one-way behavior and reviewing explicit 202 propagation.\nAdd deterministic regression coverage for both arrival orders and concurrent delivery,\nthen validate `WsReliableMessagingTest#testTwowayMessageLoss` and\n`WsReliableMessagingIT#testTwowayMessageLoss` in JVM and native modes.\nNo fix has been implemented or validated yet.\n\nThe equivalent unconditional assignment is also present in\n[Apache CXF 4.2.4 `HTTPConduit`](https://github.com/apache/cxf/blob/cxf-4.2.4/rt/transports/http/src/main/java/org/apache/cxf/transport/http/HTTPConduit.java#L1724)\nand warrants an upstream fix.\n\n\u003cdetails>\n\u003csummary>Standalone component reproducer\u003c/summary>\n\nSave as `Late202Reproducer.java`.\nCompile and run with the built `extensions/core/runtime/target/classes` directory and that module's\ndependencies on the classpath.\nThe package declaration permits access to the package-private response handler.\nThe reproducer deliberately expects the current defect; it must be adapted to assert preservation for a regression test.\n\n```java\npackage io.quarkiverse.cxf.vertx.http.client;\n\nimport java.io.InputStream;\nimport java.lang.reflect.Proxy;\nimport java.net.URI;\nimport java.util.Arrays;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport javax.xml.namespace.QName;\nimport io.vertx.core.MultiMap;\nimport io.vertx.core.http.HttpClientRequest;\nimport io.vertx.core.http.HttpClientResponse;\nimport org.apache.cxf.Bus;\nimport org.apache.cxf.endpoint.Client;\nimport org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl;\nimport org.apache.cxf.endpoint.Endpoint;\nimport org.apache.cxf.message.Exchange;\nimport org.apache.cxf.message.ExchangeImpl;\nimport org.apache.cxf.message.Message;\nimport org.apache.cxf.message.MessageImpl;\nimport org.apache.cxf.service.model.BindingOperationInfo;\nimport org.apache.cxf.service.model.BindingInfo;\nimport org.apache.cxf.service.model.EndpointInfo;\nimport org.apache.cxf.service.model.MessageInfo;\nimport org.apache.cxf.service.model.OperationInfo;\nimport org.apache.cxf.transport.http.Cookies;\n\n/** Diagnostic: real response handler and CXF result processing, controlled delivery order. */\npublic class Late202Reproducer {\n    static final String NS = \"https://quarkiverse.github.io/quarkiverse-docs/quarkus-cxf/test/ws-rm\";\n    static final String RESULT = \"WS-ReliableMessaging Hello Paul! counter: 3\";\n\n    @SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\")\n    static \u003cT> T proxy(Class\u003cT> type, java.lang.reflect.InvocationHandler handler) {\n        return (T) Proxy.newProxyInstance(type.getClassLoader(), new Class\u003c?>[] {type}, handler);\n    }\n\n    static class ResultReader extends ClientImpl {\n        ResultReader(Endpoint endpoint) {\n            super(proxy(Bus.class, (p, m, a) -> null), endpoint);\n        }\n        Object[] read(Message out, Exchange exchange, BindingOperationInfo operation) throws Exception {\n            return processResult(out, exchange, operation, null);\n        }\n    }\n\n    static void run(boolean replyBefore202) throws Exception {\n        EndpointInfo info = new EndpointInfo();\n        info.setBinding(new BindingInfo(null, \"http://schemas.xmlsoap.org/wsdl/soap/\"));\n        info.setProperty(\"org.apache.cxf.ws.addressing.MAPAggregator.decoupledDestination\", new Object());\n        Endpoint endpoint = proxy(Endpoint.class, (p, m, a) -> switch (m.getName()) {\n            case \"getEndpointInfo\" -> info;\n            case \"size\" -> 0;\n            case \"entrySet\" -> java.util.Set.of();\n            default -> null;\n        });\n        Exchange exchange = new ExchangeImpl();\n        exchange.put(Endpoint.class, endpoint);\n        exchange.put(Client.KEEP_CONDUIT_ALIVE, true);\n        Message out = new MessageImpl();\n        out.put(Message.INBOUND_MESSAGE, false);\n        exchange.setOutMessage(out);\n        Message reply = new MessageImpl();\n        reply.setContent(List.class, List.of(RESULT));\n\n        HttpClientRequest request = proxy(HttpClientRequest.class, (p, m, a) ->\n            m.getName().equals(\"absoluteURI\") ? \"http://localhost:8080/services/wsrm\" : null);\n        HttpClientResponse response = proxy(HttpClientResponse.class, (p, m, a) -> switch (m.getName()) {\n            case \"request\" -> request;\n            case \"statusCode\" -> 202;\n            case \"statusMessage\" -> \"Accepted\";\n            case \"headers\" -> MultiMap.caseInsensitiveMultiMap().add(\"Content-Length\", \"0\");\n            default -> null;\n        });\n        Runnable deliverReply = () -> {\n            synchronized (exchange) {\n                exchange.setInMessage(reply);\n                exchange.put(ClientImpl.FINISHED, true);\n                exchange.notifyAll();\n            }\n        };\n        if (replyBefore202) deliverReply.run();\n        new VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.ResponseHandler(\n                URI.create(\"http://localhost:8080/services/wsrm\"), out, new Cookies(),\n                msg -> { throw new AssertionError(\"Empty 202 should not reach observer\"); })\n            .handle(new VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.ResponseEvent(response, InputStream.nullInputStream()));\n        if (!replyBefore202) deliverReply.run();\n\n        OperationInfo operation = new OperationInfo();\n        operation.setName(new QName(NS, \"hello\"));\n        MessageInfo output = operation.createMessage(new QName(NS, \"helloResponse\"), MessageInfo.Type.OUTPUT);\n        output.addMessagePart(new QName(NS, \"helloResponse\")).setConcreteName(new QName(NS, \"helloResponse\"));\n        operation.setOutput(\"helloResponse\", output);\n        BindingOperationInfo binding = new BindingOperationInfo(null, operation);\n        System.out.println(\"Ordering: \" + (replyBefore202 ? \"SOAP reply -> HTTP 202\" : \"HTTP 202 -> SOAP reply\"));\n        System.out.println(\"Reply preserved: \" + (exchange.getInMessage() == reply));\n        try {\n            Object[] result = new ResultReader(endpoint).read(out, exchange, binding);\n            System.out.println(\"CXF result: \" + Arrays.toString(result));\n            if (replyBefore202) throw new AssertionError(\"Expected late 202 to overwrite result\");\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            if (!replyBefore202 || !e.getClass().getSimpleName().equals(\"IllegalEmptyResponseException\")) throw e;\n            System.out.println(\"CXF failure: \" + e);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {\n        run(false);\n        run(true);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n\u003c/details>\n\n_AI-generated by Codex on behalf of [Peter class="avatarLink__sqWzwe prc-Link-Link-9ZwDx" data-component="Link" href="https://github.com/ppalaga-agent" aria-label="@ppalaga-agent&#x27;s profile"><img data-component="Avatar" narrowViewportWrapper__Uis-SQ"><div class="titleSection__PUvpxr"><a class="authorLoginLink__51Riy1 prc-Link-Link-9ZwDx" data-component="Link" href="https://github.com/ppalaga-agent" data-testid="issue-body-header-author">ppalaga-agent</a></div><div class="footer__7ecx1m footerSection__kzv2bq"><span>opened </span><a class="dateLink__IC75fj prc-Link-Link-9ZwDx" data-component="Link" href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/2415#issue-6354853525" data-testid="issue-body-header-link"><relative-time class="RelativeTime__isT-DQ" data-component="RelativeTime">on Nov 12, 2026</relative-time></a></div></div><div class="narrowViewportWrapper__C5cNkS actionsWrapper__rTb725"><div class="edits__KjKzVq editsSection__3L_O_u"></div><div class="badgesSection__IjiaLx"><div class="badgeGroup__ZvfmMk"></div><div class="actionsSection__QKxX29"><button data-component="IconButton" type="button" aria-haspopup="true" aria-expanded="false" tabindex="0" class="prc-Button-ButtonBase-9n-Xk prc-Button-IconButton-fyge7" data-loading="false" data-no-visuals="true" data-size="small" data-variant="invisible" aria-labelledby="_R_fqlmkjmr9mm5_" id="_R_qlmkjmr9mm5_"><svg data-component="Octicon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" class="octicon octicon-kebab-horizontal" viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="16" height="16" fill="currentColor" display="inline-block" overflow="visible" style="vertical-align:text-bottom"><path d="M8 9a1.5 1.5 0 1 0 0-3 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 0 3ZM1.5 9a1.5 1.5 0 1 0 0-3 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 0 3Zm13 0a1.5 1.5 0 1 0 0-3 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 0 3Z"></path></svg></button><span class="prc-TooltipV2-Tooltip-tLeuB" data-direction="s" data-component="Tooltip" aria-hidden="true" id="_R_fqlmkjmr9mm5_">Issue body actions</span></div></div></div></div></div><div class="IssueBody__1iGjHw" id="issue-body-viewer" data-testid="issue-body-viewer"><div data-testid="markdown-body" data-team-hovercards-enabled="true" class="markdown-body" data-turbolinks="false"><div class="markdown-body safe-html-box__Uwc6Al Container__ZAxOBo"><p dir="auto"><code class="notranslate">VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.ResponseHandler</code> can overwrite a completed decoupled SOAP response<br>
when the empty HTTP 202 acknowledgement for the original request is processed afterward.<br>
The client then throws <code class="notranslate">ClientImpl$IllegalEmptyResponseException</code> despite having received a valid response.<br>
The result should remain available regardless of the order in which the two responses arrive.</p>
<p dir="auto">This defect was found while investigating the native WS-ReliableMessaging failure in<br>
<a href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/actions/runs/43142815034/job/130933581016">workflow run 43142815034, job 130933581016</a>.<br>
Related integration tracking issue: <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5488805144" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/2293" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/2293/hovercard" href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/2293">#2293</a>.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Quarkus CXF: <code class="notranslate">3.40.4-SNAPSHOT</code>, based on commit <code class="notranslate">b12c15a1f01cfa3e685a5be2f36efe9f7dc14f93</code>.</li>
<li>Quarkus: <code class="notranslate">3.40.999-SNAPSHOT</code>, commit <code class="notranslate">93c7ca2c408c97dcb5bd919a3e30fd0475dfb2cf</code>.</li>
<li>Apache CXF: <code class="notranslate">4.2.4</code>.</li>
<li>Native builder: Mandrel <code class="notranslate">25.0.5.0-Final</code>, JDK <code class="notranslate">25.0.5+8-LTS</code>; Maven/test harness used Java 17.</li>
<li>Failing test: <code class="notranslate">WsReliableMessagingIT.testTwowayMessageLoss</code>, calling <code class="notranslate">hello("Paul")</code>.<br>
Expected HTTP 200, received HTTP 500.<br>
The JVM counterpart passed, and native compilation succeeded.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The application log shows Paul's request at <code class="notranslate">03:45:37.483</code>, a correctly correlated <code class="notranslate">helloResponse</code><br>
containing <code class="notranslate">WS-ReliableMessaging Hello Paul! counter: 3</code> at <code class="notranslate">03:45:37.487</code>,<br>
and the following failure at <code class="notranslate">03:45:37.489</code>:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="jakarta.xml.ws.soap.SOAPFaultException: Response message did not contain proper response data.
Expected: {https://quarkiverse.github.io/quarkiverse-docs/quarkus-cxf/test/ws-rm}helloResponse
Caused by: org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl$IllegalEmptyResponseException
    at org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl.processResult(ClientImpl.java:675)"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code class="notranslate">jakarta.xml.ws.soap.SOAPFaultException: Response message did not contain proper response data.
Expected: {https://quarkiverse.github.io/quarkiverse-docs/quarkus-cxf/test/ws-rm}helloResponse
Caused by: org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl$IllegalEmptyResponseException
    at org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl.processResult(ClientImpl.java:675)
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">In the empty/partial-response branch,<br>
<a href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/blob/b12c15a1f01cfa3e685a5be2f36efe9f7dc14f93/extensions/core/runtime/src/main/java/io/quarkiverse/cxf/vertx/http/client/VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.java#L1467"><code class="notranslate">ResponseHandler.handle()</code> unconditionally calls <code class="notranslate">exchange.setInMessage(inMessage)</code></a>.<br>
The preceding decoupled-endpoint check guards callback removal, but does not guard this assignment.<br>
If the SOAP reply has already populated the exchange and marked it finished,<br>
the late HTTP 202 replaces that reply with an empty message.<br>
<code class="notranslate">ClientImpl.processResult()</code> then finds no decoded result and throws the exception above.</p>
<p dir="auto">A focused local reproducer invokes the real compiled <code class="notranslate">ResponseHandler</code> and CXF 4.2.4 <code class="notranslate">ClientImpl.processResult()</code><br>
with controlled delivery order and stubbed HTTP objects.<br>
The compiled handler's source was checked against the failing 3.40 commit and is identical.<br>
It produces:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Ordering: HTTP 202 -&gt; SOAP reply
Reply preserved: true
CXF result: [WS-ReliableMessaging Hello Paul! counter: 3]
Ordering: SOAP reply -&gt; HTTP 202
Reply preserved: false
CXF failure: org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl$IllegalEmptyResponseException: Response message did not contain proper response data. Expected: {https://quarkiverse.github.io/quarkiverse-docs/quarkus-cxf/test/ws-rm}helloResponse"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code class="notranslate">Ordering: HTTP 202 -&gt; SOAP reply
Reply preserved: true
CXF result: [WS-ReliableMessaging Hello Paul! counter: 3]
Ordering: SOAP reply -&gt; HTTP 202
Reply preserved: false
CXF failure: org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl$IllegalEmptyResponseException: Response message did not contain proper response data. Expected: {https://quarkiverse.github.io/quarkiverse-docs/quarkus-cxf/test/ws-rm}helloResponse
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This confirms the overwrite defect and reproduces the exact exception.<br>
Attribution of this particular CI failure to that ordering is strongly supported, but remains an inference:<br>
the CI log does not trace the empty HTTP 202 handler or the exchange assignments.<br>
The reproducer is a component-level diagnostic, not a native end-to-end reproduction.</p>
<p dir="auto">The <a href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/actions/runs/42432252214/job/128583513744">November 8 run</a><br>
failed on the same Paul call with the same exception.<br>
The <a href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/actions/runs/42940456485/job/130260744312">November 11 run</a><br>
passed with the same Quarkus commit, consistent with a timing-dependent failure.</p>
<p dir="auto">Proposed fix: prevent empty transport acknowledgements from replacing the application response<br>
for decoupled exchanges, while preserving ordinary one-way behavior and reviewing explicit 202 propagation.<br>
Add deterministic regression coverage for both arrival orders and concurrent delivery,<br>
then validate <code class="notranslate">WsReliableMessagingTest#testTwowayMessageLoss</code> and<br>
<code class="notranslate">WsReliableMessagingIT#testTwowayMessageLoss</code> in JVM and native modes.<br>
No fix has been implemented or validated yet.</p>
<p dir="auto">The equivalent unconditional assignment is also present in<br>
<a href="https://github.com/apache/cxf/blob/cxf-4.2.4/rt/transports/http/src/main/java/org/apache/cxf/transport/http/HTTPConduit.java#L1724">Apache CXF 4.2.4 <code class="notranslate">HTTPConduit</code></a><br>
and warrants an upstream fix.</p>
<details>
<summary>Standalone component reproducer</summary>
<p dir="auto">Save as <code class="notranslate">Late202Reproducer.java</code>.<br>
Compile and run with the built <code class="notranslate">extensions/core/runtime/target/classes</code> directory and that module's<br>
dependencies on the classpath.<br>
The package declaration permits access to the package-private response handler.<br>
The reproducer deliberately expects the current defect; it must be adapted to assert preservation for a regression test.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="package io.quarkiverse.cxf.vertx.http.client;

import java.io.InputStream;
import java.lang.reflect.Proxy;
import java.net.URI;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
import javax.xml.namespace.QName;
import io.vertx.core.MultiMap;
import io.vertx.core.http.HttpClientRequest;
import io.vertx.core.http.HttpClientResponse;
import org.apache.cxf.Bus;
import org.apache.cxf.endpoint.Client;
import org.apache.cxf.endpoint.ClientImpl;
import org.apache.cxf.endpoint.Endpoint;
import org.apache.cxf.message.Exchange;
import org.apache.cxf.message.ExchangeImpl;
import org.apache.cxf.message.Message;
import org.apache.cxf.message.MessageImpl;
import org.apache.cxf.service.model.BindingOperationInfo;
import org.apache.cxf.service.model.BindingInfo;
import org.apache.cxf.service.model.EndpointInfo;
import org.apache.cxf.service.model.MessageInfo;
import org.apache.cxf.service.model.OperationInfo;
import org.apache.cxf.transport.http.Cookies;

/** Diagnostic: real response handler and CXF result processing, controlled delivery order. */
public class Late202Reproducer {
    static final String NS = &quot;https://quarkiverse.github.io/quarkiverse-docs/quarkus-cxf/test/ws-rm&quot;;
    static final String RESULT = &quot;WS-ReliableMessaging Hello Paul! counter: 3&quot;;

    @SuppressWarnings(&quot;unchecked&quot;)
    static &lt;T&gt; T proxy(Class&lt;T&gt; type, java.lang.reflect.InvocationHandler handler) {
        return (T) Proxy.newProxyInstance(type.getClassLoader(), new Class&lt;?&gt;[] {type}, handler);
    }

    static class ResultReader extends ClientImpl {
        ResultReader(Endpoint endpoint) {
            super(proxy(Bus.class, (p, m, a) -&gt; null), endpoint);
        }
        Object[] read(Message out, Exchange exchange, BindingOperationInfo operation) throws Exception {
            return processResult(out, exchange, operation, null);
        }
    }

    static void run(boolean replyBefore202) throws Exception {
        EndpointInfo info = new EndpointInfo();
        info.setBinding(new BindingInfo(null, &quot;http://schemas.xmlsoap.org/wsdl/soap/&quot;));
        info.setProperty(&quot;org.apache.cxf.ws.addressing.MAPAggregator.decoupledDestination&quot;, new Object());
        Endpoint endpoint = proxy(Endpoint.class, (p, m, a) -&gt; switch (m.getName()) {
            case &quot;getEndpointInfo&quot; -&gt; info;
            case &quot;size&quot; -&gt; 0;
            case &quot;entrySet&quot; -&gt; java.util.Set.of();
            default -&gt; null;
        });
        Exchange exchange = new ExchangeImpl();
        exchange.put(Endpoint.class, endpoint);
        exchange.put(Client.KEEP_CONDUIT_ALIVE, true);
        Message out = new MessageImpl();
        out.put(Message.INBOUND_MESSAGE, false);
        exchange.setOutMessage(out);
        Message reply = new MessageImpl();
        reply.setContent(List.class, List.of(RESULT));

        HttpClientRequest request = proxy(HttpClientRequest.class, (p, m, a) -&gt;
            m.getName().equals(&quot;absoluteURI&quot;) ? &quot;http://localhost:8080/services/wsrm&quot; : null);
        HttpClientResponse response = proxy(HttpClientResponse.class, (p, m, a) -&gt; switch (m.getName()) {
            case &quot;request&quot; -&gt; request;
            case &quot;statusCode&quot; -&gt; 202;
            case &quot;statusMessage&quot; -&gt; &quot;Accepted&quot;;
            case &quot;headers&quot; -&gt; MultiMap.caseInsensitiveMultiMap().add(&quot;Content-Length&quot;, &quot;0&quot;);
            default -&gt; null;
        });
        Runnable deliverReply = () -&gt; {
            synchronized (exchange) {
                exchange.setInMessage(reply);
                exchange.put(ClientImpl.FINISHED, true);
                exchange.notifyAll();
            }
        };
        if (replyBefore202) deliverReply.run();
        new VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.ResponseHandler(
                URI.create(&quot;http://localhost:8080/services/wsrm&quot;), out, new Cookies(),
                msg -&gt; { throw new AssertionError(&quot;Empty 202 should not reach observer&quot;); })
            .handle(new VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.ResponseEvent(response, InputStream.nullInputStream()));
        if (!replyBefore202) deliverReply.run();

        OperationInfo operation = new OperationInfo();
        operation.setName(new QName(NS, &quot;hello&quot;));
        MessageInfo output = operation.createMessage(new QName(NS, &quot;helloResponse&quot;), MessageInfo.Type.OUTPUT);
        output.addMessagePart(new QName(NS, &quot;helloResponse&quot;)).setConcreteName(new QName(NS, &quot;helloResponse&quot;));
        operation.setOutput(&quot;helloResponse&quot;, output);
        BindingOperationInfo binding = new BindingOperationInfo(null, operation);
        System.out.println(&quot;Ordering: &quot; + (replyBefore202 ? &quot;SOAP reply -&gt; HTTP 202&quot; : &quot;HTTP 202 -&gt; SOAP reply&quot;));
        System.out.println(&quot;Reply preserved: &quot; + (exchange.getInMessage() == reply));
        try {
            Object[] result = new ResultReader(endpoint).read(out, exchange, binding);
            System.out.println(&quot;CXF result: &quot; + Arrays.toString(result));
            if (replyBefore202) throw new AssertionError(&quot;Expected late 202 to overwrite result&quot;);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            if (!replyBefore202 || !e.getClass().getSimpleName().equals(&quot;IllegalEmptyResponseException&quot;)) throw e;
            System.out.println(&quot;CXF failure: &quot; + e);
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        run(false);
        run(true);
    }
